For cauchy-theorem / cauchy-formula / residue modes, `circleAnimCompleted` is false → early return → drag never starts. Single finger (touch pointer) presses the box and nothing happens.Fix: use the `drag-enabled` class as the draggable criterion, and add `touch-action: none` to the follower CSS.

## 问题根源

动画完成时，`circleAnimLoop` 里的执行顺序出了问题：

1. 先把 `circleAnimCompleted = true`，并给积分框加上 `drag-enabled` 类（允许拖动）；
2. 但紧接着的"存入历史"代码块又把 `circle = null`、**`circleAnimCompleted` 重置回 `false`**；
3. 而积分框的 `pointerdown` 处理器用 `!circleAnimCompleted` 作为拦截条件——在 Cauchy Thm / Cauchy Formula / Residue 模式下，手指按下时这个条件成立，直接 `return`，拖拽还没开始就被拦截了。

## 修复内容（共 2 处）

1. **判定条件修正**：`pointerdown` 的拦截条件改为检查 `drag-enabled` 类（它只在动画真正完成后才添加，且不会被复位），不再依赖会被清掉的 `circleAnimCompleted` 标志；同时补了一个动画进行中的拦截，避免拖动与动画帧互相抢位置。
2. **CSS 补充**：给 `#integralFollower` 加上 `touch-action: none`，确保浏览器不会把单指按住滑动手势接管为页面滚动/缩放手势，触摸拖动更可靠。

已用无头浏览器实测验证：画圆 → 动画完成 → 单指按住积分框拖动，积分框精确跟随手指位移（dx=100, dy=50 完全吻合），其他模式（Taylor/Laurent）的既有拖动行为不受影响。[01092026052510.HTML](sandbox:///mnt/agents/output/01092026052510.HTML)

## 原因

这是**重复除以 2π** 的 bug：

- Cauchy Formula 模式下，动画结算时积分结果**已经做过一次** `1/(2πi)` 归算（代码里 `re: sumIm/(2π), im: -sumRe/(2π)`），存入历史的 `pc.result` 本身就是 f(a) 的估计值；
- 但渲染 "Previous contours" 历史列表时，又对 `pc.result` **再除了一次 2π**。

所以历史值 = 真实 f(a) ÷ 2π。用你的截图验证：0.90 ÷ 2π ≈ 0.143 → 显示 `+0.14`，0.10 ÷ 2π ≈ 0.016 → 显示 `-0.02i`，完全吻合。修复后测试：放置 a、画两条包围 a 的围道，积分框显示 `f(a) = +0.95-0.05i ✓ matches f(a)`，历史 `C1: f(a) = +0.95-0.05i`，与理论值 `0.95-0.05i` 一致。另外说明一点：历史记录显示的是**每条围道结算那一刻**的 f(a) 估计值——如果你事后拖动了 a 点，旧记录不会跟着变（它们记录的是当时的结果），这是正常行为。[01092026052510.HTML](sandbox:///mnt/agents/output/01092026052510.HTML)

```js
      // 把再前一个已完成的圈也一并删掉。

        circle = null;
        circleDrawing = false;
        circleAnimCompleted = false;
        circleSamples = [];
        circleSampleIntegrals = [{re:0, im:0}];
        flashState = null;
        flashPanel.style.display = 'none';
        integralFollower.style.display = 'none';
        integralFollower.classList.remove('drag-enabled');
        updateHint();
        render();
        return;
      }
      if(pastCircles.length > 0){
        pastCircles.pop();
        if(resultPanels.length > 0){
          resultPanels[resultPanels.length - 1].remove();
          resultPanels.pop();
        }
        circle = null;
        circleDrawing = false;
        circleAnimating = false;
        circleAnimCompleted = false;
        circleSamples = [];
        circleSampleIntegrals = [{re:0, im:0}];
        flashState = null;
        flashPanel.style.display = 'none';
        integralFollower.style.display = 'none';
        integralFollower.classList.remove('drag-enabled');
        updateHint();
        render();
      }
    });
  }
```

Confirmed the bug. The undo handler:

1. `circleAnimating = false;`
2. `if(pastCircles.length > 0){ pastCircles.pop(); ... circle = null; ... }`

Problem: a just-drawn circle that hasn't completed its animation (e.g., flashing red because it encloses a singularity — the "photon can't move" case — or mid-animation) lives in `circle`, NOT in `pastCircles` (it's only pushed to pastCircles when the animation completes). The handler unconditionally pops `pastCircles` (removing the *previous* completed circle) AND nulls `circle` (removing the current one) — two circles gone at once.Fix: if `circle` exists (a not-yet-saved circle), remove only it; otherwise pop the last completed one from pastCircles.Also noticed: at startup, undoBtn is display:none even though initial mode is cauchy-theorem — it only appears after switching modes. 修复完成，三种场景全部测试通过。✅

## 问题根源

圆圈的生命周期分两段：刚画好时光点动画未结束，圈存在 `circle` 变量里；**动画跑完后**才会转入 `pastCircles` 历史数组。而原来的 Undo 逻辑是"无脑"同时做两件事——`pastCircles.pop()` 加 `circle = null`：

- **光点动画正常完成的圈**：已转入 `pastCircles`，`circle` 本身就是空的，点 Undo 只弹出一个 → 表现正常；
- **光点不动的圈**（比如包围了奇点、正在红色闪烁告警的圈，或动画进行中的圈）：它还在 `circle` 里、**尚未进** `pastCircles`，点 Undo 时 `circle = null` 删掉了它，但 `pastCircles.pop()` 又把**再前一个**已完成的圈也弹掉了 → 一次删两个。

## 修复内容

1. **Undo 改为两段式判断**：当前存在未入库的圈（`circle` 非空）时，只撤销它——清掉闪烁状态、动画状态、积分框，不动 `pastCircles`；当前没有圈时，才弹出 `pastCircles` 里最近完成的一个。
2. **附带小修复**：Undo 按钮原先启动时是隐藏的（初始就是 Cauchy Thm 模式，但按钮要等切换一次模式才显示），现在启动即显示。

自动化测试验证（f(z)=1/z）：

- 场景1：A 完成 → B 包围极点闪烁 → Undo → A 完好保留 ✅
- 场景2：A 完成 → Undo → A 被正确移除 ✅
- 场景3：A 完成 → B 动画进行中 Undo → 只取消 B，A 保留 ✅

## 回答你的问题

围道检测其实**没有出错**——图上 z=-1 被正确识别为包围（黄色高亮、显示 n=1·Res=0）。问题出在**内置留数表把这个函数的留数数值写错了**：对 f(z)=1/(z²-1)²，z=-1 是二阶极点，真实留数应为： Resz=−1=dzd[(z−1)21]z=−1=(z−1)3−2z=−1=+41 所以 ∮ = 2πi·(1/4) = **+1.57i**——你屏幕上数值积分算出的 +1.57i 一直是对的！错的是代码里把 z=±1 两个极点的留数硬编码成了 0（正确应为 z=1 → **−1/4**，z=-1 → **+1/4**，两者之和为 0，与无穷远处留数为 0 自洽）。

2. **1/(z³-1)**：z=ω → ω/3 = −1/6 + (√3/6)i，z=ω² → ω²/3 = −1/6 − (√3/6)i（原来都错写成实数 1/3，正确留数是复数）

其余条目（1/z、1/z²、z+1/z、sin(z)/z、1/(z²-1)、1/(z²(z-1))、z/(z²-1) 等）逐一核对无误。实测验证：围 z=-1 → `Σn·Res=+0.25，✓ matches`；围双极点 → `Σn·Res=0，∮=0，✓ matches`；围 z=ω → `Σn·Res=-0.17+0.29i，✓ matches`。[01092026052510.HTML](sandbox:///mnt/agents/output/01092026052510.HTML)

Is that correct? It should be correct, because the two circles are not continuous (not connected), right?"Yes — the user is correct. The integral box shows the result of the LATEST contour only (the one just animated). Each circle is a separate, independent closed contour; the app computes and displays the integral for the most recently drawn circle, not the sum over all circles. Since the two circles are disjoint (not a single continuous path), there's no mathematical reason to sum them — each contour integral is an independent quantity. The residue theorem applies per-contour: each circle here encloses z=-1 with Res=+0.5, so each gives 2πi·0.5 = +3.14i (πi ≈ 3.1416i). Both circles individually give the same value +3.14i, and the displayed result matches the latest one.So answer: yes, correct. The panel always shows the most recent contour's integral. The two circles are independent contours, not one continuous path, so their integrals aren't summed. Both happen to enclose z=-1 only, so each yields 2πi·0.5 = πi ≈ 3.14i — same number, which might make it look like "one circle's result" but either circle alone gives exactly this.Note: if the user wanted the sum over a multi-component contour (e.g., an annulus boundary with outer CCW + inner CW), that would be a single continuous path with opposite orientations — the app doesn't support composite contours; each drawn circle is treated independently.Also worth mentioning: historically, "Previous contours" comparison was only in cauchy-theorem/cauchy-formula modes' panels, not residue mode. The residue panel shows only the latest. That's by design.No code change needed — this is a question, not a bug. Answer concisely and confirm their reasoning. ## 解释

积分框显示的是**最近一次画的那条围道**的积分结果，不是屏幕上所有圈的总和。这在数学上是合理的：

- 你画的这两个圈是**两条各自独立的闭合围道**，并不是一条连续的路径。围道积分 ∮_C 是相对于**单条**闭合路径定义的，每条围道各自独立地满足留数定理；
- 这两个圈恰好都只包围 z=-1（Res=+0.5），所以无论算哪一个，结果都是 2πi·0.5 = πi ≈ **+3.14i**。两个圈各自的结果相同，所以显示值看起来"只是一个圈的结果"——但它本来就等于其中任何一个圈的结果，∮=+3.14i、Σn·Res=+0.50、✓ matches 是自洽的。

什么时候"两个圈"才需要加起来算？只有当它们构成**一条复合围道**时——比如环形区域（圆环）的边界：外圈逆时针 + 内圈顺时针，中间由割线连接成一条连续路径，此时积分是外圈减内圈。你这两个圈是同心的、方向相同、互不相连，不满足这个条件，所以不能相加。当前设计就是"每条围道独立计算、面板显示最新一条"（历史结果在 Cauchy Thm / Cauchy Formula 模式的 Previous contours 里对比）。如果你希望留数模式下也能看到每条历史围道各自的结果列表，或者想支持"外圈+内圈反向"的复合围道求和，告诉我，我可以加上。

## 修改内容

**1. 无效围道闪烁两下即消失**
闪烁时长从 3 秒（闪 4 下）缩短为 1.6 秒、只闪 2 下，随后围道自动消失，可立即画新圈。**2. loop 控制器在留数定理模式恢复**
显示逻辑恢复为仅 Residue Thm 模式出现，按钮设置（1~9 圈、到界变暗）与之前完全相同。**3. 围道完成后保持激活，光点支持顺/逆时针追加**
这是核心改动。之前动画一结束圈就被存入历史、光点采样被清空，导致 +/- 的追加逻辑成为死代码。现在留数模式下：

- 动画完成后圈保持激活，**光点停留在围道上**；
- 按 **+**：光点从当前位置**逆时针**再跑一圈，积分累加，n+1；
- 按 **−**：光点**顺时针**退一圈，积分扣减，n−1——正是你说的"逆时针两圈再经割线顺时针一圈"的净绕数模型；
- 积分框实时刷新并始终 `✓ matches`（实测：n=1 → +6.28i，+ → n=2 → +12.57i，+ → n=3 → +18.85i，− → 回到 n=2 → +12.57i）。

**4. 画新圈时旧圈自动归档**
在有激活圈的情况下直接画新圈即可：旧圈变为彩色虚线历史圈，新圈从 n=loopCount 重新开始（loopCount 沿用当前设置，与之前语义一致）。Undo 行为不变：先撤当前激活圈，再撤历史圈。回归测试：Cauchy Thm 多圈历史、Cauchy Formula 均不受影响。✅[01092026052510.HTML](sandbox:///mnt/agents/output/01092026052510.HTML)

Let me think about the design and whether it's mathematically sound.**Mathematical analysis:**The residue theorem for a composite contour: ∮_C f dz = 2πi Σ_k n(C, a_k) Res(f, a_k), where n(C, a_k) is the winding number of C around a_k. So a contour that winds +3 around A, −4 around B, 0 around others gives 2πi(3·Res_A − 4·Res_B). The user's model is exactly right — this is the general residue theorem with winding numbers.How to construct such a curve? A standard construction: for each pole a_k with winding n_k, draw a small circle around a_k traversed n_k times (sign = direction), connected by "corridors" (cut lines) that go out and back — the connecting segments are traversed in opposite directions so their contributions cancel. The classic "keyhole"/"comb" contour: start at base point, go to circle A (line out), loop A n times, come back along the same line (line back cancels), go to B, etc. The straight segments cancel pairwise, leaving Σ 2πi·n_k·Res_k.Implementation approach for the animation:

- The integral accumulates: line segments cancel if traversed forth and back — but if we go A→B directly (not backtracking), the segments DON'T cancel. Hmm.

Then connectors contribute 0 to the integral exactly (traversed in both directions), and winding is purely n_j per pole. This is the standard textbook construction (like the proof of the residue theorem for multiple poles).Alternative: a "chain" contour where connectors cancel approximately. To keep it clean and mathematically exact, the comb/keyhole construction is best: choose a basepoint (maybe the first pole's circle edge, or a central point), and for each pole, the path goes from basepoint to the pole's circle along a line, loops n_k times, returns along the same line. Adjacent poles' connectors share the basepoint, so the path is continuous: ...loop A n times, back along line A to base, out along line B, loop B n times, back to base, ... end at base. Closed curve. Connectors cancel exactly (same segment, opposite directions). Integral = Σ 2πi n_k Res_k exactly. function buildResidueContour(){
```js
  const poles = getResiduePoles().filter(sp => (poleLoops[sp.label]||0) !== 0);
  if(poles.length === 0) return null;

  // path samples:
  samples = [];
  function addLine(p1, p2, n){ for i=1..n: lerp }
  function addCircle(c, r, startAngle, loops, dir, n){ total angle = 2π*loops*dir from startAngle }
  
  order poles: by descending angle around centroid? or by x. Use as-is order.
  cur = B
  for each pole k:
    E = entry point (angle thetaE = atan2(B.y-a.y, B.x-a.x))
    addLine(cur, E)
    mark.startIdx = samples.length
    addCircle(a_k, r_k, thetaE, |n_k|, sign(n_k))  // CCW positive: angle increases
    mark.endIdx = samples.length
    addLine(E, B)  // return along same line → cancels
    cur = B
  // integrals cumulative via midpoint
}
```

```js
  function getResiduePoles(){
    const f = getCurrentFunc();
    return f.singularityPoints.filter(sp =>
      !sp.isInf && (sp.type === 'pole' || sp.type === 'essential') && computeResidue(funcType, sp));
  }

  function getPoleLoop(sp){ return poleLoops[sp.label] || 0; }

  function updatePoleLoopControls(){
    const container = document.getElementById('poleLoops');
    // clear
    container.innerHTML = '';
    if(appMode !== 'residue'){ container.style.display='none'; return; }
    const poles = getResiduePoles();
    if(poles.length === 0){ container.style.display='none'; return; }
    container.style.display='block';
    for(const sp of poles){
      const s = toScreen(sp.x, sp.y);
      const div = document.createElement('div');
      div.className = 'pole-loop';
      div.style.left = s.x + 'px';
      div.style.top = (s.y + 16) + 'px';
      const n = getPoleLoop(sp);
      div.innerHTML = '<button class="pl-btn" data-d="-1">−</button><span class="pl-val">' + (n>0?'+':'') + n + '</span><button class="pl-btn" data-d="1">+</button>';
      div.querySelectorAll('.pl-btn').forEach(btn => {
        btn.addEventListener('click', function(ev){
          ev.stopPropagation();
          if(residueAnimating) return;
          let v = getPoleLoop(sp) + parseInt(this.getAttribute('data-d'));
          v = Math.max(-5, Math.min(5, v));
          poleLoops[sp.label] = v;
          // 设置变化 → 清除旧动画结果
          residuePath = null; poleContribs = {}; residueAnimCompleted = false;
          integralFollower.style.display = 'none';
          integralFollower.classList.remove('drag-enabled');
          updatePoleLoopControls();
          render();
        });
      });
      container.appendChild(div);
    }
  }
```

JavaScript 复制

```js
  function buildResidueContour(){
    const poles = getResiduePoles().filter(sp => getPoleLoop(sp) !== 0);
    if(poles.length === 0) return null;
    const all = getResiduePoles();
    // 每个留点的小圆半径：不超过到最近其他奇点距离的 0.4，且 ≤ 0.35
    const items = poles.map(sp => {
      let dmin = Infinity;
      for(const q of getCurrentFunc().singularityPoints){
        if(q === sp || q.isInf) continue;
        dmin = Math.min(dmin, Math.hypot(q.x - sp.x, q.y - sp.y));
      }
      let r = 0.35;
      if(dmin < Infinity) r = Math.min(r, dmin * 0.4);
      return {sp, r};
    });
    // 基点 B：所有小圆右侧
    let maxX = -Infinity, sumY = 0;
    for(const it of items){ maxX = Math.max(maxX, it.sp.x + it.r); sumY += it.sp.y; }
    const B = {x: maxX + 0.7, y: sumY / items.length};

    const samples = [];   // {z}
    const marks = [];     // {label, s, e}
    const f = getCurrentFunc();
    function push(z){ samples.push({z}); }
    function addLine(p1, p2){
      const n = 60;
      for(let i = 1; i <= n; i++){
        push({x: p1.x + (p2.x-p1.x)*i/n, y: p1.y + (p2.y-p1.y)*i/n});
      }
    }
    let cur = {x: B.x, y: B.y};
    push({x: B.x, y: B.y});
    for(const it of items){
      const sp = it.sp, r = it.r, n = getPoleLoop(sp);
      const thE = Math.atan2(B.y - sp.y, B.x - sp.x);
      const E = {x: sp.x + r*Math.cos(thE), y: sp.y + r*Math.sin(thE)};
      addLine(cur, E);                       // 去程割线
      const mStart = samples.length - 1;
      const dir = n > 0 ? 1 : -1;            // 正=逆时针
      const loops = Math.abs(n);
      const cn = 400 * loops;
      for(let k = 1; k <= cn; k++){
        const t = thE + dir * 2 * Math.PI * loops * k / cn;
        push({x: sp.x + r*Math.cos(t), y: sp.y + r*Math.sin(t)});
      }
      const mEnd = samples.length - 1;
      marks.push({label: sp.label, s: mStart, e: mEnd, sp, n});
      addLine(E, B);                          // 回程割线（与去程抵消）
      cur = {x: B.x, y: B.y};
    }
    // 累计积分（中点法）
    const cum = [{re:0, im:0}];
    for(let k = 0; k < samples.length - 1; k++){
      const s1 = samples[k].z, s2 = samples[k+1].z;
      const dzRe = s2.x - s1.x, dzIm = s2.y - s1.y;
      const mz = {x:(s1.x+s2.x)/2, y:(s1.y+s2.y)/2};
      const fz = f.map(mz);
      let segRe = 0, segIm = 0;
      if(!fz.inf && isFinite(fz.x) && isFinite(fz.y)){
        segRe = fz.x*dzRe - fz.y*dzIm;
        segIm = fz.x*dzIm + fz.y*dzRe;
      }
      const prev = cum[cum.length-1];
      cum.push({re: prev.re + segRe, im: prev.im + segIm});
    }
    // 理论值 2πi·Σn·Res
    let sumRes = {x:0, y:0};
    for(const m of marks){
      const res = computeResidue(funcType, m.sp);
      sumRes.x += m.n * res.x; sumRes.y += m.n * res.y;
    }
    const theo = {x: -2*Math.PI*sumRes.y, y: 2*Math.PI*sumRes.x};
    const total = cum[cum.length-1];
    const totalLoops = items.reduce((a,it)=>a+Math.abs(getPoleLoop(it.sp)),0);
    return {samples, cum, marks, total, theo, sumRes, totalLoops, connectors: items.length*2};
  }
```

**Animation loop:**

JavaScript 复制

```js
  function startResidueContour(){
    residuePath = buildResidueContour();
    if(!residuePath){ updateHint(); return; }
    poleContribs = {};
    residueAnimating = true;
    residueAnimCompleted = false;
    residueAnimStartTime = performance.now();
    residueAnimDuration = residuePath.totalLoops * 1100 + residuePath.connectors * 250;
    integralFollower.style.display = 'block';
    hintLabel.style.display = 'none';
    requestAnimationFrame(residueAnimLoop);
  }

  function residueAnimLoop(now){
    if(!residueAnimating || !residuePath) return;
    const elapsed = now - residueAnimStartTime;
    const progress = Math.min(1, elapsed / residueAnimDuration);
    const idx = Math.min(residuePath.samples.length - 1, Math.floor(progress * (residuePath.samples.length - 1)));
    residuePhotonIdx = idx;
    const val = residuePath.cum[idx];
    // 更新已完成的留点贡献
    for(const m of residuePath.marks){
      if(idx >= m.e && !poleContribs[m.label]){
        const c0 = residuePath.cum[m.s], c1 = residuePath.cum[m.e];
        poleContribs[m.label] = {re: c1.re - c0.re, im: c1.im - c0.im};
      }
    }
    // follower 实时显示
    integralFormula.innerHTML = '<span style="font-size:18px; font-style:italic;">∮</span><span class="int-c">C</span><span style="font-style:italic;">f(z)dz</span> ' + (progress < 1 ? '≈' : '=');
    integralValue.textContent = formatComplex({x: val.re, y: val.im}, 2);
    integralValue.className = '';
    const sc = residuePath.samples[idx].screen;
    if(sc){ integralFollower.style.left = (sc.x + 14) + 'px'; integralFollower.style.top = (sc.y - 52) + 'px'; }
    integralFollower.style.display = 'block';
    render();
    if(progress < 1){
      requestAnimationFrame(residueAnimLoop);
    } else {
      residueAnimating = false;
      residueAnimCompleted = true;
      integralFollower.classList.add('drag-enabled');
      // 最终结果
      const tot = residuePath.total, theo = residuePath.theo;
      const err = Math.hypot(tot.re - theo.x, tot.im - theo.y);
      const matchStr = err < 0.05 ? '<span style="color:#4ade80;">✓ matches</span>' : '<span style="color:#facc15;">err=' + err.toFixed(3) + '</span>';
      let html = '<div style="font-size:13px; font-style:italic; white-space:nowrap;">';
      html += '<span style="font-size:18px; font-style:italic;">∮</span><span class="int-c">C</span><span style="font-style:italic;">f(z)dz</span> = ' + formatComplex({x:tot.re, y:tot.im}, 2) + '</div>';
      html += '<div style="margin-top:4px; color:#facc15; font-size:11px; font-family:monospace;">Σn·Res = ' + formatComplex(residuePath.sumRes, 2) + '</div>';
      html += '<div style="color:#facc15; font-size:11px; font-family:monospace;">2πi·Σn·Res = ' + formatComplex({x:theo.x, y:theo.y}, 2) + ' &nbsp;|&nbsp; ' + matchStr + '</div>';
      integralFormula.innerHTML = html;
      integralValue.textContent = '';
      integralFollower.style.display = 'block';
      render();
    }
  }
```

Wait — the error threshold: with many loops, integration error grows; err<0.05 might be tight for ±5 loops. Contribution per loop for 1/z is 2πi≈6.28; integration with 400 pts/loop midpoint → error tiny (~1e-6). Connectors cancel exactly. So err small. But for functions like inv_z2m1_sq with sharper variation, still fine at r=0.35 circles (400 pts/loop). Keep 0.05 but maybe scale: err < 0.05 * max(1,totalLoops)? Let me use tolerance 0.02*totalLoops + 0.03... simpler: `err < 0.05 * Math.max(1, residuePath.totalLoops/2)`. Meh — use 0.05*max(1,totalLoops) is too loose (5 loops → 0.25). I'll compute and see in tests; start with 0.05*Math.max(1, totalLoops*0.4) eh. Let me just use relative: err < 0.01 * max(1, |theo|) + 0.02.   function drawResidueContour(){
```js
    if(appMode !== 'residue' || !residuePath) return;
    const upto = residueAnimating ? residuePhotonIdx : residuePath.samples.length - 1;
    // compute screens lazily
    ctx.beginPath();
    let first = true, prevS = null;
    for(let i = 0; i <= upto; i++){
      const smp = residuePath.samples[i];
      const p = mapCirclePoint(smp.z);
      if(p.inf || !isFinite(p.x) || !isFinite(p.y)){ first = true; prevS = null; continue; }
      const s = toScreen(p.x, p.y);
      smp.screen = s;
      if(first){ ctx.moveTo(s.x, s.y); first = false; }
      else {
        const d = prevS ? Math.hypot(s.x-prevS.x, s.y-prevS.y) : 0;
        if(d > 120) ctx.moveTo(s.x, s.y); else ctx.lineTo(s.x, s.y);
      }
      prevS = s;
    }
    ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(0, 255, 200, 0.75)';
    ctx.lineWidth = 2.5;
    ctx.setLineDash([6,4]);
    ctx.stroke();
    ctx.setLineDash([]);
    // 光点
    const smp = residuePath.samples[upto];
    if(smp && smp.screen){
      const s = smp.screen;
      for(let i = 4; i >= 0; i--){ ...glow... }
      ...photon dot...
    }
  }
```

这个设计我认为**非常好**——它正是留数定理的一般形式 ∮_C f dz = 2πi·Σ n(C,aₖ)·Res(f,aₖ)，每个留点独立的绕数（含负值顺时针）才是完整的故事；手绘确实画不出"A +3 圈 → B −4 圈 → … → 回到起点"的复合围道。我先回答你的两个问题，然后实现它。**关于闪烁的误会**：上一轮我把"重复的无效围道"理解成了"包围分支点的无效围道"（那个已改成闪两下消失）；你说的"重复围道"是指在同一留点上重复画圈。当时我为了支持"留数模式画多个圈 + loop 追加"把旧圈改成了彩色历史圈。按你这次的设计，**留数模式直接禁用手绘画圈**，重复围道的问题就彻底不存在了。**实现方案**（关键数学点）：复合围道采用"锁眼/割线"构造——从公共基点出发沿直线到某留点小圆、绕 nₖ 圈、**沿同一直线返回**，往返割线严格抵消，所以总积分精确等于 Σ 2πi·nₖ·Resₖ，每个留点绕数独立。分支点（多值函数）和 ∞ 极点不参与；本性奇点留数已知的（如 e^{1/z}）可以参与。开始实现。先加 CSS 和每留点 stepper 容器：

JavaScript 复制

```js
// completion branch, after integralFollower.style.display = 'block';
let lx = parseFloat(integralFollower.style.left) || 0;
let ly = parseFloat(parseFloat(integralFollower.style.top)) || 0;
const fw = integralFollower.offsetWidth, fh = integralFollower.offsetHeight;
lx = Math.max(4, Math.min(window.innerWidth - fw - 4, lx));
ly = Math.max(4, Math.min(window.innerHeight - fh - 4, ly));
integralFollower.style.left = lx + 'px';
```

修好了。问题在于动画第一帧的时间戳：浏览器的帧同步时刻可能略早于点击 Animate 时记录的开始时间，算出的进度是一个极小的负数，导致取路径样本时下标变成 −1，第一帧就抛异常，整条动画链直接断掉——所以看起来"没有动画开始"。这个错误是时序相关的，在桌面端偶发，在触屏设备上更容易触发。现在进度和下标都做了钳位。修复后完整验证了一遍（全部通过）：

- **z=-1 设 +2、z=1 设 −1**（1/(z²−1)²）：连跑 8 次全部成功，∮ = +4.71i，Σn·Res = +0.75，✓ matches；每个留点下方留下贡献值（+3.14i 和 +1.57i，正好是各自的 2πi·n·Res）
- **三留点混合绕圈**（1/(z³−1)，z=1 设 +1、z=ω 设 −2、z=ω² 设 +3）：∮ = +9.07+1.05i，与理论值 5π√3/3 + πi/3 完全一致
- **Undo**：清除已生成的围道动画，保留各留点的圈数设置，方便微调后重新 Animate
- **Reset**：清空所有圈数设置和围道
- **留数模式下手指画圈已禁用**，提示语显示"Set loops (−5..+5) for each pole, then tap Animate"
- 柯西定理、柯西公式模式回归正常

另外还发现并修了一个显示问题：复合围道的基点在最右留点之外，动画结束时积分框停在屏幕右缘会被裁掉一部分，现在结束后会自动钳位回屏幕内（仍然可以单指拖动）。[01092026052510.HTML](sandbox:///mnt/agents/output/01092026052510.HTML)

JavaScript 复制

```js
// 从 x 轴正半轴方向开始，按逆时针顺序访问留点，轨迹更清晰
const cx0 = items.reduce((a,it)=>a+it.sp.x,0)/items.length;
const cy0 = items.reduce((a,it)=>a+it.sp.y,0)/items.length;
items.sort((a,b) => {
  const aa = (Math.atan2(a.sp.y-cy0, a.sp.x-cx0) + 2*Math.PI) % (2*Math.PI);
  const bb = (Math.atan2(b.sp.y-cy0, b.sp.x-cx0) + 2*Math.PI) % (2*Math.PI);
  return aa - bb;
});
```

Let me write it:

JavaScript 复制

```js
function drawResidueContour(){
  if(appMode !== 'residue' || !residuePath) return;
  const P = residuePath;
  const upto = residueAnimating ? residuePhotonIdx : P.samples.length - 1;
  const LOOP_COLORS = ['#22d3ee', '#f472b6', '#a3e635', '#fb923c', '#c084fc'];

  // 先计算每个样本点的屏幕坐标（经过网格变形映射）
  const scr = [];
  for(let i = 0; i <= upto; i++){
    const p = mapCirclePoint(P.samples[i].z);
    if(p.inf || !isFinite(p.x) || !isFinite(p.y)){ scr.push(null); P.samples[i].screen = null; continue; }
    const s = toScreen(p.x, p.y);
    P.samples[i].screen = s;
    scr.push(s);
  }

  // 判断样本 index 是否属于某留点的绕圈段，是则返回 {m, loopIdx}
  function circleSeg(idx){ // idx = 终点样本下标（>=1）
    for(const m of P.marks){
      if(idx > m.s && idx <= m.e){
        return {m, loopIdx: Math.floor((idx - m.s - 1) / 400)};
      }
    }
    return null;
  }

  // 割线段：暗青色虚线，一次描边
  ctx.beginPath();
  let prevS = null;
  for(let i = 0; i < upto; i++){
    if(circleSeg(i+1)) { prevS = null; continue; }
    const s1 = scr[i], s2 = scr[i+1];
    if(!s1 || !s2){ prevS = null; continue; }
    if(!prevS || Math.hypot(s2.x-s1.x, s2.y-s1.y) > 120 || Math.hypot(s1.x-prevS.x, s1.y-prevS.y) > 120){
      ctx.moveTo(s1.x, s1.y);
    } else {
      ctx.lineTo(s1.x, s1.y);
    }
    ctx.lineTo(s2.x, s2.y);
    prevS = s2;
  }
  ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(0, 255, 200, 0.45)';
  ctx.lineWidth = 2;
  ctx.setLineDash([6, 4]);
  ctx.stroke();
  ctx.setLineDash([]);

  // 绕圈段：每圈一种颜色，实线加粗
  for(const m of P.marks){
    const loops = Math.abs(m.n);
    for(let j = 0; j < loops; j++){
      const a = m.s + j * 400, b = m.s + (j+1) * 400;
      if(upto <= a) break;
      const end = Math.min(b, upto);
      ctx.beginPath();
      let prev = null, started = false;
      for(let i = a; i <= end; i++){
        const s = scr[i];
        if(!s){ started = false; prev = null; continue; }
        if(!started || (prev && Math.hypot(s.x-prev.x, s.y-prev.y) > 120)) ctx.moveTo(s.x, s.y);
        else ctx.lineTo(s.x, s.y);
        started = true; prev = s;
      }
      ctx.strokeStyle = LOOP_COLORS[j % LOOP_COLORS.length];
      ctx.lineWidth = 2.5;
      ctx.stroke();
    }
  }

  // 光点（不变）
  const smp = P.samples[upto];
  if(smp && smp.screen){ ... }
}
```

Wait — there's a subtlety with the loop start: loop j's first point is sample a = m.s + j*400 which is the entry point E (for j=0) or the closing point of previous loop (same location E). Drawing from a to end inclusive is correct.Note scr computed only up to upto — for i in a..end, end ≤ upto, fine.Photon position: smp.screen — set in the scr loop. But if scr[i] is null (inf), screen=null — guard exists.One more consideration: 400 points per loop is hardcoded in buildResidueContour (cn = 400*loops). I should reference the same constant. Let me define a shared const RES_LOOP_PTS = 400 at the top of the residue block and use in both places.**Also the label z=ω overlap**: in the screenshot, the label "z=ω (order 1)" is above the marker — fine.Now about the CCW sort — where exactly in buildResidueContour: after `if(items.length === 0) return null;` and before computing B. Also B's y = mean of sp.y — for symmetric cases it's 0 → B on x-axis. Good.Also, user said "最后回到原点" — the path returns to B at the end already (after last pole's addLine(E,B)).
```js
for(let k = 1; k <= cn; k++){
  const loopIdx = Math.floor((k-1) / RES_LOOP_PTS);
  const rr = r * (1 + 0.12 * loopIdx);
  const t = thE + dir * 2π * loops * k / cn;
  push({x: sp.x + rr*cos(t), y: sp.y + rr*sin(t)});
}
```

Currently no check (existed before, worked). Keep as is.Implementation of point-to-segment distance:

JavaScript 复制

```js
function segDist(px, py, x1, y1, x2, y2){
  const dx = x2-x1, dy = y2-y1;
  const L2 = dx*dx+dy*dy;
  let t = L2 ? ((px-x1)*dx + (py-y1)*dy)/L2 : 0;
  t = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, t));
  return Math.hypot(px-(x1+t*dx), py-(y1+t*dy));
}
```

```js
  }
  for(const it of items){
    const E = entry point of it (depends on H);
    for(const q of f.singularityPoints){
      if(q.isInf || q === it.sp) continue;
      if(segDist(q.x, q.y, H.x, H.y, E.x, E.y) < 0.25) return false;  // spoke shouldn't pass near other singularities
    }
  }
  return true;
}
```

JavaScript 复制

```js
    // 从 x 轴正半轴方向开始，绕质心按逆时针顺序访问留点，最后回到起点 B——
    // 各割线呈扇形展开、互不交叉，轨迹更清晰
    const cx0 = items.reduce((a, it) => a + it.sp.x, 0) / items.length;
    const cy0 = items.reduce((a, it) => a + it.sp.y, 0) / items.length;
    items.sort((a, b) => {
      const aa = (Math.atan2(a.sp.y - cy0, a.sp.x - cx0) + 2 * Math.PI) % (2 * Math.PI);
      const bb = (Math.atan2(b.sp.y - cy0, b.sp.x - cx0) + 2 * Math.PI) % (2 * Math.PI);
      return aa - bb;
    });

    let maxX = -Infinity, sumY = 0;
    for(const it of items){ maxX = Math.max(maxX, it.sp.x + it.r); sumY += it.sp.y; }
    const B = {x: maxX + 0.7, y: sumY / items.length};
```

```js
    // 选择"轮毂"基点 B：优先原点（对称留点组的自然中心，如 1/(z³-1)、1/(z²-1)），
    // 其次所选留点的质心，最后退回最右留点右侧。
    // 要求：B 不在任何奇点小圆内/附近，且每条辐条（B→E_k）不掠过其他奇点——
    // 这样 B→E_k→绕圈→E_k→B 的往返割线严格抵消，总积分精确等于 Σ 2πi·n_k·Res_k。
    const cx0 = items.reduce((a, it) => a + it.sp.x, 0) / items.length;
    const cy0 = items.reduce((a, it) => a + it.sp.y, 0) / items.length;
    let maxX = -Infinity, sumY = 0;
    for(const it of items){ maxX = Math.max(maxX, it.sp.x + it.r); sumY += it.sp.y; }
    const Bfar = {x: maxX + 0.7, y: sumY / items.length};

    function segDist(px, py, x1, y1, x2, y2){
      const dx = x2 - x1, dy = y2 - y1, L2 = dx*dx + dy*dy;
      let t = L2 ? ((px - x1)*dx + (py - y1)*dy) / L2 : 0;
      t = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, t));
      return Math.hypot(px - (x1 + t*dx), py - (y1 + t*dy));
    }
    function entryOf(it, H){
      const dx = H.x - it.sp.x, dy = H.y - it.sp.y, d = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
      return {x: it.sp.x + it.r * dx / d, y: it.sp.y + it.r * dy / d};
    }
    function hubOK(H){
      for(const q of f.singularityPoints){
        if(q.isInf) continue;
        if(Math.hypot(H.x - q.x, H.y - q.y) < 0.3) return false;
      }
      for(const it of items){
        if(Math.hypot(H.x - it.sp.x, H.y - it.sp.y) < it.r + 0.25) return false;
        const E = entryOf(it, H);
        for(const q of f.singularityPoints){
          if(q.isInf || q === it.sp) continue;
          if(segDist(q.x, q.y, H.x, H.y, E.x, E.y) < 0.25) return false;
        }
      }
      return true;
    }
    let B;
    if(hubOK({x: 0, y: 0})) B = {x: 0, y: 0};
    else if(hubOK({x: cx0, y: cy0})) B = {x: cx0, y: cy0};
    else B = Bfar;

    // 从 x 轴正半轴方向开始，绕基点 B 按逆时针顺序访问留点，最后回到 B——
    // 各辐条呈扇形展开、互不交叉，轨迹更清晰
    items.sort((a, b) => {
      const aa = (Math.atan2(a.sp.y - B.y, a.sp.x - B.x) + 2 * Math.PI) % (2 * Math.PI);
      const bb = (Math.atan2(b.sp.y - B.y, b.sp.x - B.x) + 2 * Math.PI) % (2 * Math.PI);
      return aa - bb;
    });
```

Let me double-check residues for the quintic: g(z) = (z⁴−1)(z−2) = z⁵−2z⁴−z+2. g'(z) = 5z⁴−8z³−1.

- g'(1) = 5−8−1 = −4 → Res = −1/4 ✓
- g'(−1) = 5+8−1 = 12 → Res = 1/12 ✓
- g'(2) = 80−64−1 = 15 → Res = 1/15 ✓
- g'(i) = 5·1−8·(−i)−1 = 4+8i → Res = 1/(4+8i) = (4−8i)/(16+64) = (4−8i)/80 = 0.05−0.1i ✓
- g'(−i) = 4−8i → Res = 0.05+0.1i ✓

Map for 1/(z⁹−1): compute z⁹. General power: use polar or repeated multiplication. Simple: compute via complex exponentiation: r = |z|, θ = atan2; z⁹ = r⁹(cos9θ, sin9θ). Then 1/(w−1). Straightforward:

JavaScript 复制

```js
map: function(z){
  const r = Math.hypot(z.x, z.y), th = Math.atan2(z.y, z.x);
  const r9 = Math.pow(r, 9), a = 9*th;
  const wx = r9*Math.cos(a) - 1, wy = r9*Math.sin(a);
  const d = wx*wx + wy*wy;
  if(d < 1e-10) return {x:1e6, y:0, inf:true};
  return {x:wx/d, y:-wy/d};
}
```

Map for quintic: 1/((z²−1)(z−2)(z²+1)) = 1/((z⁴−1)(z−2)). Compute z⁴ via z² squared:

JavaScript 复制

```js
map: function(z){
  const z2x = z.x*z.x - z.y*z.y, z2y = 2*z.x*z.y;
  const z4x = z2x*z2x - z2y*z2y - 1, z4y = 2*z2x*z2y;   // z⁴−1
  const wx = z4x*(z.x-2) - z4y*z.y, wy = z4x*z.y + z4y*(z.x-2);  // (z⁴−1)(z−2)
  const d = wx*wx + wy*wy;
  if(d < 1e-10) return {x:1e6, y:0, inf:true};
  return {x:wx/d, y:-wy/d};
}
```

The only real dangers: (a) spoke passes THROUGH a pole (f → inf, integration skips segments → error), (b) spoke passes very close to a pole (huge |f|, midpoint rule error spikes). Threshold 0.25 from any other pole handles both reasonably. Crossing another pole's circle is fine mathematically (the circle is just ink). Function display name for quintic: 1/((z²−1)(z−2)(z²+1)) — factored form shows the poles clearly. Good for teaching.**Edit 3 — function definitions** (after z2p1_inv):

JavaScript 复制

```js
    inv_z9m1: {
      name: '1/(z⁹-1)', latexHtml: ...,
      category: '🟡 亚纯-九个简单极点-有理函', categoryColor: '#facc15',
      singularities: 'z=e^{2πik/9} (k=0..8): simple poles',
      singularityPoints: (function(){
        const SUP = ['','','²','³','⁴','⁵','⁶','⁷','⁸'];
        const pts = [{x:1, y:0, type:'pole', label:'z=1', order:1}];
        for(let k = 1; k <= 8; k++){
          const a = 2 * Math.PI * k / 9;
          pts.push({x: Math.cos(a), y: Math.sin(a), type:'pole', label:'z=ζ' + SUP[k], order:1});
        }
        return pts;
      })(), hasBranchCut: false,
      map: function(z){
        const r = Math.hypot(z.x, z.y), th = Math.atan2(z.y, z.x);
        const r9 = Math.pow(r, 9), a = 9 * th;
        const wx = r9 * Math.cos(a) - 1, wy = r9 * Math.sin(a);
        const d = wx * wx + wy * wy;
        if(d < 1e-10) return {x:1e6, y:0, inf:true};
        return {x:wx/d, y:-wy/d};
      }
    },
    inv_quintic: {
      name: '1/((z²-1)(z-2)(z²+1))', latexHtml: ...,
      category: '🟡 亚纯-五个简单极点-有理函（非对称）', categoryColor: '#facc15',
      singularities: 'z=±1, 2, ±i: simple poles',
      singularityPoints: [
        {x:1, y:0, type:'pole', label:'z=1', order:1},
        {x:-1, y:0, type:'pole', label:'z=-1', order:1},
        {x:2, y:0, type:'pole', label:'z=2', order:1},
        {x:0, y:1, type:'pole', label:'z=i', order:1},
        {x:0, y:-1, type:'pole', label:'z=-i', order:1}
      ], hasBranchCut: false,
      map: function(z){
        const z2x = z.x*z.x - z.y*z.y, z2y = 2*z.x*z.y;
        const u4x = z2x*z2x - z2y*z2y - 1, u4y = 2*z2x*z2y;     // z⁴−1
        const wx = u4x*(z.x-2) - u4y*z.y, wy = u4x*z.y + u4y*(z.x-2);  // (z⁴−1)(z−2)
        const d = wx*wx + wy*wy;
        if(d < 1e-10) return {x:1e6, y:0, inf:true};
        return {x:wx/d, y:-wy/d};
      }
    },
```

latexHtml: follow the existing pattern with spans. For inv_z9m1: copy inv_z3m1's latexHtml with 3→9. For quintic, build: f(z)=1/((z²-1)(z-2)(z²+1)). **Edit 9 — stepper rows + compact**:
Add helper:

JavaScript 复制

```js
  // 留点按绕质心辐角排序后的序号（奇偶分行，避免留点密集时 stepper 互相遮挡）
  function getPoleRow(sp){
    const poles = getResiduePoles();
    if(poles.length <= 4) return 0;
    const cx = poles.reduce((a,p)=>a+p.x,0)/poles.length;
    const cy = poles.reduce((a,p)=>a+p.y,0)/poles.length;
    const sorted = poles.slice().sort((a,b) => {
      const aa = (Math.atan2(a.y-cy, a.x-cx) + 2*Math.PI) % (2*Math.PI);
      const bb = (Math.atan2(b.y-cy, b.x-cx) + 2*Math.PI) % (2*Math.PI);
      return aa - bb;
    });
    const idx = sorted.findIndex(p => p.label === sp.label);
    return idx < 0 ? 0 : idx % 2;
  }
```

Spoke to z=1: from (3.05,0) to E(1)=(1.35,0) — passes through (2,0) = pole z=2 EXACTLY. f.map at (2,0) → inf → those midpoint samples are skipped (the `if(!fz.inf...)` guard skips the segment contribution). The integral along the spoke skips a few segments near z=2 — out and back skip the SAME segments → still cancels! And the result: T4 all+1 gave ∮=0 ✓ matches. Because the out/back cancellation holds even with skipped segments (both directions skip identically). And T5 also ✓. So even the "bad" hub gives correct results here — but risky in general (if a spoke passes near but not exactly through a pole, midpoint error could be large and NOT cancel... actually it does cancel: out and back sample the SAME midpoints (addLine identical points, reversed). The cum segments are exact negations regardless of f values — wait no! The segments are skipped if f is inf — segment k out and corresponding segment back: out segment from p_i to p_{i+1}, back segment from p_{i+1} to p_i — midpoint is the same point, f value same, dz negated → contributions are exact negations (both included or both skipped together). So cancellation is EXACT for any spoke, even through poles!!Wait, then why did I bother with hubOK at all?? The concern was: spoke passes very close to a pole → |f| huge → the RUNNING integral (live display and per-pole contributions) swings wildly (like the −220 seen earlier), and worse — the contribution stamps: cum[m.e]−cum[m.s] only spans the circle loops, so spokes don't affect stamps. The total: exact cancellation → correct. The only real problem is the live display showing huge transient values and potential visual mess (photon path through a pole → grid deformation explodes → mapCirclePoint inf → drawing breaks). A spoke through a pole looks bad visually (path goes through the singularity marker). So hubOK is still worth it for clean visuals, but the math is robust regardless. JavaScript 复制

```js
    let B = null;
    const hubCandidates = [{x: 0, y: 0}, {x: cx0, y: cy0}];
    for(const dist of [3.5, 5.5]){
      for(let k = 0; k < 16; k++){
        const a = 2 * Math.PI * k / 16;
        hubCandidates.push({x: cx0 + dist * Math.cos(a), y: cy0 + dist * Math.sin(a)});
      }
    }
    hubCandidates.push(Bfar);
    for(const c of hubCandidates){ if(hubOK(c)){ B = c; break; } }
    if(!B) B = Bfar;
```

全部完成并验证通过：**Loop 范围 −7..+7**：按钮在 ±7 处钳位（测试确认 +7/−7 都到位），颜色也扩到 7 种（青、粉、绿、橙、紫、琥珀、红）。圈数很多时动画会自动加速（如 9 留点全设 7 圈共 63 圈，每圈从 1.1s 压到 0.45s，避免等太久）。**两个新函数**（都在函数列表里，🟡 亚纯类）：

1. **1/(z⁹−1)** — 9 个简单极点，均匀分布在单位圆上（对称的那个）。留数直接由极点坐标给出 Res = p/9。测试 z=1 设 +2、z=ζ⁴ 设 −1：∮ = +0.24+2.05i，✓ matches。9 个 stepper 会分两行交错摆放并自动缩小，默认缩放级别下还是有点挤，双指放大后就很宽松。
2. **1/((z²−1)(z−2)(z²+1))** — 5 个简单极点 z = ±1, 2, ±i（**非对称**的那个，z=2 打破了旋转对称）。留数：−1/4、1/12、1/15、0.05∓0.1i。这个函数有个很妙的性质：五个留数之和恰好为 0，全部设 +1 时 ∮ = 0（测试通过）——总和为零但每个留点的贡献（+0.52i、−1.57i、+0.42i、±0.63+0.31i）都清楚地留在各自下方，很适合讲"局部贡献与总体抵消"。

非对称函数还带来一个我之前没处理的问题：它的十字形极点布局使得原点、质心、正东/正南/对角等方向的基点的辐条都会穿过其他极点。现在基点是自动搜索的——先试原点和质心，再按距离递增扫描 16 个方向的环形候选，第一个辐条不碰到任何极点的位置胜出（这个函数最终选在了 (1.25, 0.85)，从截图看辐条干净地扇形展开）。回归测试全部通过：1/(z³−1)、1/(z²−1)²、e^{1/z}（原点在奇点上时自动用环形候选基点）。[01092026052510.HTML](sandbox:///mnt/agents/output/01092026052510.HTML)

But drawSingularities draws only when... it draws for all singularityPoints in view. The connector should only show in residue mode for poles with steppers (eligible poles). Condition: appMode === 'residue' && computeResidue(...). Same condition as the Res text. Draw the line right after the Res text.Implementation:

JavaScript 复制

```js
if(appMode === 'residue' && (sp.type === 'pole' || sp.type === 'essential')){
  const res = computeResidue(funcType, sp);
  if(res){
    const row = getPoleRow(sp);
    const stepTop = s.y + 26 + row * 44;
    // 从标记到 stepper 的细虚线，放缩/分行时也能看清归属
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(s.x, s.y + r + 2);
    ctx.lineTo(s.x, stepTop - 3);
    ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(250,204,21,0.35)';
    ctx.lineWidth = 1;
    ctx.setLineDash([3, 3]);
    ctx.stroke();
    ctx.setLineDash([]);
    ctx.fillStyle = '#facc15';
    ctx.font = '9px monospace';
    ctx.fillText('Res=' + formatComplex(res, 2), s.x, s.y + r + 12);
    const cb = poleContribs[sp.label];
    if(cb){
      ctx.fillStyle = '#4ade80';
      ctx.font = 'bold 10px monospace';
      ctx.fillText('∮=' + formatComplex({x: cb.re, y: cb.im}, 2), s.x, stepTop + 42);
    }
  }
}
```

JavaScript 复制

```js
// 收集本帧所有 stepper 的目标位置，水平错开重叠
const items = poles.map(sp => ({sp, s: toScreen(sp.x, sp.y)}));
items.sort((a,b) => (a.s.y - b.s.y) || (a.s.x - b.s.x));
const w = poles.length > 4 ? 64 : 94; // 估计宽度
for(let i = 1; i < items.length; i++){
  const prev = items[i-1], cur = items[i];
  if(Math.abs(cur.s.y - prev.s.y) < 34){ // 同一水平带
    const need = (prev._x + w/2 + 4) - (cur._x - w/2);
    if(need > 0) cur._x += need;
  }
}
```

JavaScript 复制

```js
let poleLayout = {};  // label -> {x, top} (本帧 stepper 布局，含水平避让)
function computePoleLayout(){
  poleLayout = {};
  if(appMode !== 'residue') return;
  const poles = getResiduePoles();
  if(!poles.length) return;
  const compact = poles.length > 4;
  const w = compact ? 66 : 96;
  const items = poles.map(sp => {
    const s = toScreen(sp.x, sp.y);
    return {label: sp.label, x: s.x, top: s.y + 26, poleX: s.x, poleY: s.y};
  });
  // 同一水平带内的 stepper 若水平重叠，向右顺移避让（保持紧贴 Res 行的高度）
  items.sort((a, b) => (a.top - b.top) || (a.x - b.x));
  for(let i = 1; i < items.length; i++){
    const prev = items[i-1], cur = items[i];
    if(Math.abs(cur.top - prev.top) < 34 && cur.x - prev.x < w + 4){
      cur.x = prev.x + w + 4;
    }
  }
  for(const it of items) poleLayout[it.label] = {x: it.x, top: it.top};
}
```

Wait, greedy with sorted-by-top: after pushing cur.x right, the NEXT item in the same band compares against cur (items[i-1]) — correct chaining. JavaScript 复制

```js
let poleLayout = {};   // label -> {x, top}
function computePoleLayout(){
  poleLayout = {};
  if(appMode !== 'residue') return;
  const poles = getResiduePoles();
  if(!poles.length) return;
  const w = poles.length > 4 ? 66 : 96;   // stepper 估计宽度
  const h = 28;                            // stepper 估计高度
  const items = poles.map(sp => {
    const s = toScreen(sp.x, sp.y);
    return {label: sp.label, hx: s.x, hy: s.y + 26};
  });
  // 垂直重叠多的极点（如单位圆左侧一簇）按 top 排序，逐个放置：
  // 优先正下方，其次左右小步平移，再不行整体下移一行（行距 30px，紧贴上一行）
  items.sort((a, b) => (a.hy - b.hy) || (a.hx - b.hx));
  const placed = [];
  function overlaps(x, y){
    if(x - w/2 < 2 || x + w/2 > W - 2) return true;
    for(const p of placed){
      if(Math.abs(x - p.x) < (w + 4) && Math.abs(y - p.y) < (h + 4)) return true;
    }
    return false;
  }
  for(const it of items){
    let done = false;
    for(let row = 0; row < 8 && !done; row++){
      const y = it.hy + row * 30;
      const shifts = [0];
      for(let k = 8; k <= 96; k += 8){ shifts.push(k, -k); }
      for(const dx of shifts){
        if(!overlaps(it.hx + dx, y)){
          it.x = it.hx + dx; it.top = y; done = true; break;
        }
      }
    }
    if(!done){ it.x = Math.max(w/2, Math.min(W - w/2, it.hx)); it.top = it.hy; }
    placed.push({x: it.x, y: it.top});
    poleLayout[it.label] = {x: it.x, top: it.top};
  }
}
```

Wait — the horizontal overlap threshold: steppers centered w apart exactly touch; use < w+2 for overlap. h similar. Let me use (w+2) and (h+2).Note the w estimated 66 but actual compact ~64; +2 margin ok.For the connector line condition in drawSingularities: dist(stepX,stepTop vs s.x, s.y+26) > 6 → draws for row-displaced too. Good.Also: previous behavior for ≤4 poles (w=96): two-pole functions like 1/(z²+1) poles at ±i — no conflicts. 这是之前你设计的三步走实现泰勒展开和洛朗展开的方案，目前文件里，第一步做了一部分。
三步实施方案
🔷 Step 1：单系数探索 —— "这一项从哪来？"
核心目标：建立高阶柯西积分公式与泰勒级数单个系数的直观对应。
具体交付：
- 在 `taylor` 模式下，新增底部阶数选择器 `n = 0, 1, 2, 3...`（按钮组或滑块）
- 用户选择某个 `n` 后，放置展开中心 `a`，画围道 `C`
- 被积函数自动变为 \displaystyle \frac{f(z)}{(z-a)^{n+1}}
- 光点沿 `C` 积分动画，结果面板实时显示：
```
∮_C f(z)/(z-a)^(n+1) dz = X + Yi
↓ ÷(2πi) ÷n!
c_n = 0.25 + 0.00i ← 这就是泰勒级数中 (z-a)^n 的系数
```
- 在复平面上用单色向量场（或一组采样箭头）展示 c_n \cdot (z-a)^n 这一项在平面上的分布形态
- 不显示完整级数和，只让用户看到"第 n 项单独长什么样"
代码改动范围：
- 新增 `kernelPower` 参数到围道积分逻辑（现有 `cauchy-formula` 是 `n=0` 的特例）
- 新增阶数选择 UI（约 30 行 HTML/CSS）
- 新增 `computeTaylorCoeffByContour(n, a, circle)` 数值计算
- 新增单项向量场绘制函数
验收标准：
> 选择 n=2，画一个包围 a 的圆，能看到光点动画计算 \oint f(z)/(z-a)^3 dz，最终结果除以 2\pi i \cdot 2! 后，与理论值 f''(a)/2! 的误差 < 0.05。
---
🔷 Step 2：完整泰勒逼近 —— "加起来长什么样？"
核心目标：从"看单项"扩展到"看级数逼近效果"。
具体交付：
- 保留 Step 1 的所有功能（系数探索模式作为子选项）
- 新增 "Show Full Series" 开关/按钮
- 开启后，新增一个总阶数滑块 `N = 0, 1, 2, 3...`（最大到 8 阶即可，太高性能扛不住）
- 系统根据当前 `a` 和 `N`，一次性预计算系数表 [c_0, c_1, ..., c_N]
- 绘制两层网格：
- 背景层：原函数 f(z) 的映射网格（青色，opacity 降至 0.25）
- 前景层：泰勒多项式 P_N(z) = \sum{k=0}^N c_k(z-a)^k 的映射网格（品红/紫色，opacity 0.9）
- 可选误差可视化：在采样点上用短红线连接 f(z) 和 P_N(z)，长度代表局部误差
- 收敛半径 R 用绿色虚线圆始终显示
代码改动范围：
- 新增 `seriesApprox(z, a, coeffs)` 统一级数求值函数
- 新增 `computeTaylorCoeffs(funcType, a, N)` 系数工厂（解析公式，不用围道积分）
- 新增逼近网格绘制分支（复用现有 `drawGrid` 的采样逻辑，换颜色）
- 新增误差线绘制（可选）
验收标准：
> 对 f(z)=e^z, a=0, N=3，能看到品红色网格是 1 + z + z^2/2 + z^3/6 的映射，在 |z|<2 范围内与原函数网格基本重合；对 f(z)=1/z, a=1, N=5，能看到在 |z-1|<1 内逼近良好，圆外发散。
---
🔷 Step 3：洛朗衔接 —— "负幂次也一样"
核心目标：复用前两步的架构，支持负幂次和环形收敛域。
具体交付：
- 切换到 `laurent` 模式时，UI 自动变化：
- 正幂次滑块 `N⁺`（同 Step 2）
- 新增负幂次滑块 `N⁻ = 1, 2, 3...`
- 系数探索支持负指数：
- 选择 n = -k 时，被积函数变为 f(z) \cdot (z-a)^{k-1}
- 积分结果对应 c{-k}
- 向量场展示 c{-k} \cdot (z-a)^{-k}
- 完整逼近：P(z) = \sum{k=-N^-}^{N^+} c_k (z-a)^k
- 收敛域从单圆变为双圆：
- 外圆：|z-a| < R{out}（绿色虚线）
- 内圆：|z-a| > R{in}（橙色虚线）
- 只在环形区域绘制逼近网格
- 所有核心逻辑复用 Step 1/2 的代码，仅通过 `kernelPower` 的正负和收敛域判断做区分
代码改动范围：
- 扩展系数工厂：`computeLaurentCoeffs(funcType, a, N_pos, N_neg)`
- 扩展 `seriesApprox` 支持负幂次循环
- 修改收敛域绘制：单圆 → 双圆/环形
- 修改 `isNearSingularity` 和网格裁剪逻辑，适配环形区域
验收标准：
> 对 f(z)=e^{1/z}, a=0，选择 N^-=3, N^+=0，能看到负幂次项 z^{-1}, z^{-2}, z^{-3} 的逼近在 |z|>0 的环形区域有效；系数探索中，c{-1}=1 与理论值吻合。
---
三步之间的依赖关系
```
Step 1: 高阶核函数 + 单系数围道探索
↓
Step 2: 复用Step1的核函数逻辑，叠加完整级数渲染
↓
Step 3: 复用Step1/2的全部架构，仅扩展负幂次分支
```
每步之间我会：
1. 输出可运行的完整 HTML 文件
2. 列出该步已验证的功能清单
3. 标记"已知限制"（即留到下一步处理的问题）
4. 你确认无误后，再进入下一步

第一步（Step 1）现在补齐了。之前的文件里骨架都在（n 选择器、核函数积分、理论值对照），但有三个实质性缺陷导致它实际跑不通，外加方案本身的一处数学笔误：**本次修复**

1. **圈完成后被立即归档**（最关键的断点）：动画一结束 `circleAnimCompleted` 就被复位、结果面板被换成级数信息——导致单项 c_n(z−a)ⁿ 品红网格永远不显示、换 n 无法重算、Replay 失效。现在泰勒模式与留数模式一样保持圈激活，画新圈时才归档旧圈。
2. **数学公式多除了 n!**（方案原文的笔误）：高阶柯西公式 ∮f(z)/(z−a)ⁿ⁺¹dz = 2πi·f⁽ⁿ⁾(a)/n! = **2πi·c_n**，所以 c_n = ∮/(2πi)，不能再除 n!。原实现（及方案验收标准里的"÷(2πi·n!)"）会让结果小 n! 倍（n=2 时 0.5 vs 1.0）。已改正，面板显示 `c_n = ∮/(2πi)`。
3. **e^z 的"∞本性奇点"误判**：任何圆心靠近原点的圆都被当成"包围∞"而弹出告警、拒绝积分。泰勒模式的解析性只看有限圆盘内部，已跳过 isInf 奇点。
4. 结果面板在光点停在屏幕右侧时超出边缘——完成时和每次 render 吸附后都钳回可视区。

**验收结果**（按方案标准 + 扩展，全部通过）

- f=e^z，a=(0.5,0.3)，n=2：∮ = −1.53+4.95i，c₂ = +0.79+0.24i，理论 e^a/2! = +0.79+0.24i，**✓ 误差 < 0.05**
- 换 n=3 自动重算：c₃ = +0.26+0.08i = e^a/3! ✓
- f=1/z，a=1，n=2：c₂ = +1.00，理论 ✓
- a 在围道外：正确提示 "a not inside C → ∮ = 0 (Cauchy theorem)"
- 回归：Replay、Undo、柯西定理/公式模式归档行为、留数 Winding 动画均不受影响

**已知限制 / 与方案的偏差**

- 单项可视化用的是**品红映射网格**（与全 app 的网格变形风格一致），不是方案字面说的向量场/箭头——信息等价，如果你想换成箭头告诉我
- 展开中心 a 放置后不能拖动重定位（柯西公式模式可以），需要移动 a 目前要 Reset
- Laurent 模式仍只有收敛信息展示（那是 Step 3 的内容）

确认无误的话，我接着做 Step 2（完整泰勒逼近：N 阶滑块 + 品红逼近网格与原函数网格对比 + 收敛圆）。[01092026052510.HTML](sandbox:///mnt/agents/output/01092026052510.HTML)

完成。先回答你的问题：**Replay 是什么？** 在泰勒/洛朗模式下，画完圈后光子积分动画只播放一次。点 Replay 就是**沿当前圈重播积分过程**（用当前选的 n 重新算一遍），不用重新画圈——学生没看清积分如何累积时可以再看一次。换 n 时会自动重算，所以 Replay 纯粹是"再看一遍"。之前它没有任何说明，现在提示语里会写明它的作用。**分步英文提示**（左下角，随操作自动推进）：

| 状态 | 提示 |
| --- | --- |

| 进入泰勒模式 | **Step 1**: Tap anywhere to place the expansion center a |
| --- | --- |
| 放好 a 后 | **Step 2**: Drag to draw a circle around a — keep every singularity (✗) OUTSIDE the circle!（n 选择器下方同时显示 "Pick n here, then draw the circle"） |
| 动画进行中 | **Step 3**: The photon is computing ∮ f(z)/(z−a)ⁿ⁺¹ dz along C … |
| 完成后 | Done: c_n is in the panel, magenta grid = c_n·(z−a)^n. Tap **Replay** to re-watch, or pick another n |

**配套修复**：

- 提示语动画开始后会被隐藏、且永远不会恢复显示（updateHint 缺恢复逻辑）——已修复
- 动画完成时现在会主动刷新提示到 "Done" 状态
- 圈把奇点包进去时，警告文案改为泰勒语境："Taylor coefficients need f analytic inside C — draw a smaller circle excluding every ✗"，警告结束后提示自动回到 Step 2

10 项提示流程测试 + 数值回归全部通过。[01092026052510.HTML](sandbox:///mnt/agents/output/01092026052510.HTML)

- computeSeriesCoeffs():
  
  JavaScript 复制
  
  ```js
  function computeSeriesCoeffs(){
    seriesCoeffs = null;
    if(!pointA) return;
    const coeffs = [];
    for(let k = 0; k <= seriesN; k++){
      let c = computeNthDerivative(funcType, k, pointA);   // 解析：c_k = f⁽ᵏ⁾(a)/k!
      if(!c){ coeffs = null; break; }  // 无解析公式 → DFT 兜底
      if(c.inf){ c = {x: 1e6, y: 0}; } // hmm
      coeffs.push({x: c.x, y: c.y});
    }
    if(!coeffs) seriesCoeffs = dftCoeffs(...);
    else seriesCoeffs = coeffs;
  }
  ```
  
  DFT fallback:
  
  JavaScript 复制
  
  ```js
  function dftSeriesCoeffs(f, a, N){
    // 数值围道积分（DFT）：c_k = (1/M)Σ f(a+rω^j)ω^{-jk}，指数收敛
    const info = getConvergenceInfo(funcType, a);
    let r = (info.radius === Infinity ? 2 : info.radius * 0.7);
    if(!(r > 0)) r = 0.5;
    const M = 64;
    const coeffs = [];
    for(let k = 0; k <= N; k++) coeffs.push({x:0, y:0});
    for(let j = 0; j < M; j++){
      const t = 2*Math.PI*j/M;
      const z = {x: a.x + r*Math.cos(t), y: a.y + r*Math.sin(t)};
      const fz = f.map(z);
      if(fz.inf) return null;
      for(let k = 0; k <= N; k++){
        const w = {x: Math.cos(-k*t), y: Math.sin(-k*t)};
        coeffs[k].x += (fz.x*w.x - fz.y*w.y) / M;
        coeffs[k].y += (fz.x*w.y + fz.y*w.x) / M;
      }
    }
    return coeffs;
  }
  ```
  
  Wait — DFT formula: c_k = (1/(2πi))∮ f(z)/(z-a)^{k+1} dz. Parametrize z = a + r e^{it}, dz = ir e^{it} dt:
  c_k = (1/(2πi))∫₀^{2π} f(a+re^{it})/(r^{k+1}e^{i(k+1)t}) · ir e^{it} dt = (1/(2π))∫ f(a+re^{it}) r^{-k} e^{-ikt} dt.
  So c_k = r^{-k} · (1/M)Σ_j f(z_j) e^{-ikt_j}. I forgot r^{-k}! Include it.
- seriesApprox(z):
  
  JavaScript 复制
  
  ```js
  function seriesApprox(z){
    // Horner: c_N (z-a)^N + ... + c_0
    const dz = {x: z.x - pointA.x, y: z.y - pointA.y};
    let acc = {x: 0, y: 0};
    for(let k = seriesCoeffs.length - 1; k >= 0; k--){
      acc = {x: acc.x*dz.x - acc.y*dz.y + seriesCoeffs[k].x,
             y: acc.x*dz.y + acc.y*dz.x + seriesCoeffs[k].y};
    }
    return acc;
  }
  ```

  ```js
  function drawMappedGrid(mapFn, style, lw){
    ctx.lineWidth = lw;

      ctx.beginPath();

      for(let x = -gridRange; x <= gridRange; x += sampleStep){
        const w = mapFn({x, y});
        if(!w || w.inf || !isFinite(w.x) || !isFinite(w.y)){ first = true; continue; }
        let wx = w.x, wy = w.y;
        const mag = Math.hypot(wx, wy);
        if(mag > maxDisplay){ const k = maxDisplay/mag; wx *= k; wy *= k; }
        const s = toScreen(wx, wy);
        if(s.x < -200 || s.x > W+200 || s.y < -200 || s.y > H+200){ first = true; continue; }
        if(first){ ctx.moveTo(s.x, s.y); first = false; } else ctx.lineTo(s.x, s.y);
      }
      ctx.stroke();
    }
    ... vertical same ...
  }
  ```

```js
        hintLabel.textContent = 'Full series: cyan grid = f(z), magenta grid = P_N(z) — they coincide inside the green dashed circle (R), diverge outside';
        if(taylorInfo) taylorInfo.textContent = 'Drag to pan • pinch to zoom • adjust N';
      } else if(!circle){
        hintLabel.textContent = 'Step 2: Drag to draw a circle around a — keep every singularity (✗) OUTSIDE the circle!';
        if(taylorInfo) taylorInfo.textContent = 'Pick n here, then draw the circle';
      } else if(circleAnimCompleted){
        hintLabel.textContent = 'Done: c_n is in the panel; magenta grid = c_n·(z−a)^n. Pick another n, or draw a new circle';
        if(taylorInfo) taylorInfo.textContent = 'Tap another n — the integral recomputes automatically';
      }
```

Step 2（完整泰勒逼近）开发完成，Replay 按钮已隐藏。**Part 1 — Replay 移除**
泰勒/洛朗模式下不再显示 Mapping/Replay 按钮（换 n 自动重算，重播没有存在必要）；其他模式的 Mapping 不受影响。Done 提示语里的 Replay 引用也同步去掉了。**Part 2 — Step 2 实现**

- **子模式标签页**：Taylor 面板顶部新增 `Explore c_n | Full series P_N` 切换。Step 1 的系数探索完整保留在 Explore 页
- **Full series 页**：N = 0..8 阶数选择 + error 误差线开关；单指拖动改为平移视图（不画圈），双指捏合缩放
- **系数工厂**：优先用解析公式（c_k = f⁽ᵏ⁾(a)/k!）；无解析公式的函数自动用 **DFT 数值围道积分**兜底（在收敛圆上采样 64 点一次算出全部系数）——所以新来的 5 极点、9 极点有理函数也都能玩
- **双层网格对比**：底层青色 = f(z) 的映射网格，前景品红 = P_N(z) = Σc_k(z−a)ᵏ 的映射网格（Horner 求值），底层 z 平面网格自动调暗作参照
- **误差线**（可选）：红线连接 f(z) 与 P_N(z) 的像；误差巨大区域不画线（发散由两层网格分离本身表达），避免画面糊掉
- 绿色虚线收敛圆 R 常显；左上角函数标签变为 f(z) ≈ P_N(z)

**验收结果**（方案标准全过）：

- f=e^z，a=0，N=3：系数精确为 [1, 1, 1/2, 1/6]，P₃(1)=8/3 精确命中，|z|<2 内两层网格基本重合 ✓
- f=1/z，a=1，N=5：系数 [1,−1,1,−1,1,−1] 精确；|z−1|<1 内 P₅(1.5)=0.656 vs 真值 0.667（err 0.01），圆外 P₅(2.5)=−4.16 vs 真值 0.4 明显发散 ✓
- DFT 兜底验证：5 极点函数在 a=0 处 c₀…c₃ = 0.5, 0.25, 0.125, 0.0625，与精确级数一致到小数点后 9 位 ✓
